npm notice run as $$

npm notice run     return null;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select id, plan_name, billing_cycle_months, seat_limit, modules_enabled, status, expires_at
npm notice run   into r
npm notice run   from public.license_keys
npm notice run   where key = trim(p_key)
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run   if not found then
npm notice run     return null;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if r.status != 'unused' then
npm notice run     return null;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if r.expires_at is not null and r.expires_at < now() then
npm notice run     return null;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return jsonb_build_object(
npm notice run     'plan_name', r.plan_name,
npm notice run     'billing_cycle_months', r.billing_cycle_months,
npm notice run     'seat_limit', r.seat_limit,
npm notice run     'modules_enabled', coalesce(r.modules_enabled, '[]'::jsonb)
npm notice run   );
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;

npm notice run create or replace function public.activate_license_key(
npm notice run   p_key text,
npm notice run   p_company_name text,
npm notice run   p_industry text,
npm notice run   p_country text,

npm notice run   p_primary_contact_email text,

npm notice run )
npm notice run returns jsonb
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_uid uuid;
npm notice run   v_email text;
npm notice run   v_key_id uuid;
npm notice run   v_plan_name text;
npm notice run   v_billing_cycle integer;
npm notice run   v_seat_limit integer;
npm notice run   v_modules jsonb;
npm notice run   v_company_id uuid;
npm notice run   v_start_date date;
npm notice run   v_end_date date;
npm notice run   v_license_id uuid;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   v_uid := public.request_user_id();
npm notice run   v_email := lower(trim(public.request_user_email()));
npm notice run   if v_uid is null or v_email is null then
npm notice run     raise exception 'Not authenticated';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if lower(trim(p_primary_contact_email)) != v_email then
npm notice run     raise exception 'Primary contact email must match your account email';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if exists (select 1 from public.company_memberships where user_id = v_uid) then
npm notice run     raise exception 'Email already used in another organisation';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select id, plan_name, billing_cycle_months, seat_limit, coalesce(modules_enabled, '[]'::jsonb)
npm notice run   into v_key_id, v_plan_name, v_billing_cycle, v_seat_limit, v_modules
npm notice run   from public.license_keys
npm notice run   where key = trim(p_key) and status = 'unused'
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run   if not found then
npm notice run     raise exception 'This license has already been activated or is no longer valid. Please contact support.';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if exists (select 1 from public.license_keys where id = v_key_id and expires_at is not null and expires_at < now()) then
npm notice run     raise exception 'This license is no longer valid. Please contact support.';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   v_start_date := current_date;
npm notice run   v_end_date := v_start_date + (v_billing_cycle * interval '1 month');
npm notice run   insert into public.companies (
npm notice run     name, license_type, employee_limit, primary_admin_user_id,
npm notice run     industry, country, status, subscription_duration_months, metadata
npm notice run   )
npm notice run   values (
npm notice run     trim(p_company_name),
npm notice run     v_plan_name,
npm notice run     v_seat_limit,
npm notice run     v_uid,
npm notice run     nullif(trim(p_industry), ''),
npm notice run     nullif(trim(p_country), ''),
npm notice run     'active',
npm notice run     v_billing_cycle,
npm notice run     jsonb_build_object('primary_contact_name', nullif(trim(p_primary_contact_name), ''), 'primary_contact_phone', nullif(trim(p_phone), ''))
npm notice run   )
npm notice run   returning id into v_company_id;
npm notice run   insert into public.company_memberships (company_id, user_id, role)
npm notice run   values (v_company_id, v_uid, 'owner');
npm notice run   insert into public.org_licenses (
npm notice run     company_id, plan_name, seat_limit, start_date, end_date, status,
npm notice run     billing_cycle_months, modules_enabled, license_key_id, activated_at, activated_by_user_id, created_by_user_id
npm notice run   )
npm notice run   values (
npm notice run     v_company_id, v_plan_name, v_seat_limit,
npm notice run     v_start_date, v_end_date, 'active',
npm notice run     v_billing_cycle, v_modules, v_key_id, now(), v_uid, v_uid
npm notice run   )
npm notice run   returning id into v_license_id;
npm notice run   update public.license_keys
npm notice run   set status = 'used', used_at = now(), used_by_organization_id = v_company_id
npm notice run   where id = v_key_id;
npm notice run   begin
npm notice run     insert into public.platform_admin_audit_logs (actor_user_id, action, target_company_id, details)
npm notice run     values (v_uid, 'license_activated', v_company_id, jsonb_build_object(
npm notice run       'license_key_id', v_key_id, 'plan_name', v_plan_name, 'seat_limit', v_seat_limit
npm notice run     ));
npm notice run   exception when others then
npm notice run     null;
npm notice run   end;
npm notice run   return jsonb_build_object(
npm notice run     'organizationId', v_company_id,
npm notice run     'userId', v_uid,
npm notice run     'success', true
npm notice run   );
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;

npm notice run create or replace function public.match_document_embeddings(
npm notice run   query_embedding vector(1536),
npm notice run   match_company_id uuid,
npm notice run   match_entity_types text[] default null,
npm notice run   match_count int default 8
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns table (
npm notice run   id uuid,
npm notice run   entity_type text,
npm notice run   entity_id uuid,
npm notice run   chunk_text text,
npm notice run   metadata jsonb,
npm notice run   similarity float
npm notice run )
npm notice run language sql
npm notice run stable
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run   select
npm notice run     e.id,
npm notice run     e.entity_type,
npm notice run     e.entity_id,
npm notice run     e.chunk_text,
npm notice run     e.metadata,
npm notice run     1 - (e.embedding <=> query_embedding) as similarity
npm notice run   from public.document_embeddings e
npm notice run   where e.company_id = match_company_id
npm notice run     and (match_entity_types is null or e.entity_type = any(match_entity_types))
npm notice run     and public.is_company_member(match_company_id)
npm notice run   order by e.embedding <=> query_embedding
npm notice run   limit greatest(match_count, 1);
npm notice run $$;

npm notice run     return true;

npm notice run     if v_scope_site is null and v_scope_department is null then
npm notice run       return true;
npm notice run     end if;

npm notice run       (v_scope_site is not null and p_site_id = v_scope_site)
npm notice run       or (v_scope_department is not null and p_department_id = v_scope_department)
npm notice run     );
npm notice run   end if;

npm notice run     return true;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return false;

npm notice run 'insforge' db query create extension if not exists pgcrypto;
npm notice run alter table public.companies
npm notice run   add column if not exists code text null;
npm notice run alter table public.companies drop constraint if exists companies_code_format_check;
npm notice run alter table public.companies
npm notice run   add constraint companies_code_format_check
npm notice run   check (
npm notice run     code is null
npm notice run     or (
npm notice run       length(code) between 2 and 5
npm notice run       and code = upper(code)
npm notice run       and code ~ '^[A-Z0-9]+$'
npm notice run     )
npm notice run   );
npm notice run create unique index if not exists idx_companies_code_unique
npm notice run   on public.companies(code)
npm notice run   where code is not null;
npm notice run create or replace function public.derive_company_code(p_name text)
npm notice run returns text
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run immutable
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_name text;
npm notice run   v_code text;
npm notice run   v_word text;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   v_name := coalesce(nullif(trim(p_name), ''), '');
npm notice run   if v_name = '' then
npm notice run     return 'ORG';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   v_code := '';
npm notice run   for v_word in
npm notice run     select regexp_replace(w, '[^a-zA-Z0-9]+', '', 'g') as w
npm notice run     from regexp_split_to_table(v_name, '\s+') as w
npm notice run   loop
npm notice run     if v_word is null or v_word = '' then
npm notice run       continue;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     v_code := v_code || upper(substr(v_word, 1, 1));
npm notice run     if length(v_code) >= 5 then
npm notice run       exit;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end loop;
npm notice run   if v_code = '' then
npm notice run     v_code := upper(substr(regexp_replace(v_name, '[^a-zA-Z0-9]+', '', 'g'), 1, 5));
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if length(v_code) < 2 then
npm notice run     v_code := rpad(v_code, 2, 'X');
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if length(v_code) > 5 then
npm notice run     v_code := substr(v_code, 1, 5);
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return v_code;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run create or replace function public.ensure_company_code(p_company_id uuid)
npm notice run returns text
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_existing text;
npm notice run   v_name text;
npm notice run   v_base text;
npm notice run   v_candidate text;
npm notice run   v_suffix int;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   select c.code, c.name into v_existing, v_name
npm notice run   from public.companies c
npm notice run   where c.id = p_company_id;
npm notice run   if v_existing is not null and nullif(trim(v_existing), '') is not null then
npm notice run     return v_existing;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   v_base := public.derive_company_code(v_name);
npm notice run   v_candidate := v_base;
npm notice run   v_suffix := 2;
npm notice run   loop
npm notice run     begin
npm notice run       update public.companies
npm notice run       set code = v_candidate
npm notice run       where id = p_company_id
npm notice run         and (code is null or nullif(trim(code), '') is null);
npm notice run       select code into v_existing from public.companies where id = p_company_id;
npm notice run       if v_existing is not null then
npm notice run         return v_existing;
npm notice run       end if;
npm notice run     exception when unique_violation then
npm notice run       null;
npm notice run     end;
npm notice run     if v_suffix > 99 then
npm notice run       v_candidate := substr(v_base, 1, greatest(2, 5 - length('99'))) || '99';
npm notice run       begin
npm notice run         update public.companies set code = v_candidate where id = p_company_id and (code is null or nullif(trim(code), '') is null);
npm notice run         select code into v_existing from public.companies where id = p_company_id;
npm notice run         if v_existing is not null then
npm notice run           return v_existing;
npm notice run         end if;
npm notice run       exception when unique_violation then
npm notice run         null;
npm notice run       end;
npm notice run       raise exception 'Could not generate unique organization code for company_id=%', p_company_id;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     v_candidate := substr(v_base, 1, greatest(2, 5 - length(v_suffix::text))) || v_suffix::text;
npm notice run     v_suffix := v_suffix + 1;
npm notice run   end loop;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;

npm notice run create or replace function public.can_view_health_employee_record(p_company_id uuid, p_employee_user_id uuid)
npm notice run returns boolean
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run stable
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_role text;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if public.is_platform_admin() then
npm notice run     return true;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   v_role := public.company_role(p_company_id);
npm notice run   if v_role is null then
npm notice run     return false;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if v_role in ('owner', 'admin', 'manager', 'hr') then
npm notice run     return true;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if p_employee_user_id is null then
npm notice run     return false;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if v_role = 'employee' then
npm notice run     return p_employee_user_id = public.request_user_id();
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if v_role = 'supervisor' then
npm notice run     if p_employee_user_id = public.request_user_id() then
npm notice run       return true;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     return exists (
npm notice run       select 1
npm notice run       from public.user_profiles up
npm notice run       where up.company_id = p_company_id
npm notice run         and up.user_id = p_employee_user_id
npm notice run         and up.supervisor_user_id = public.request_user_id()
npm notice run     );
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return false;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;

npm notice run update public.hr_employee_documents
npm notice run set doc_name = coalesce(nullif(trim(doc_name), ''), title)
npm notice run where doc_name is null or trim(doc_name) = '';
npm notice run with ranked_docs as (
npm notice run   select
npm notice run     id,
npm notice run     row_number() over (
npm notice run       partition by
npm notice run         company_id,
npm notice run         employee_id,
npm notice run         lower(coalesce(doc_name, title)),
npm notice run         lower(coalesce(doc_type, '')),
npm notice run         coalesce(issue_date, date '1900-01-01')
npm notice run       order by
npm notice run         coalesce(updated_at, created_at) desc,
npm notice run         created_at desc,
npm notice run         id desc
npm notice run     ) as rn
npm notice run   from public.hr_employee_documents
npm notice run )
npm notice run delete from public.hr_employee_documents d
npm notice run using ranked_docs r
npm notice run where d.id = r.id
npm notice run   and r.rn > 1;

npm notice run create or replace function public.hr_can_access_sensitive_employee_fields(p_company_id uuid)
npm notice run returns boolean
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run stable
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_role text;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if public.is_platform_admin() then return true; end if;
npm notice run   v_role := public.company_role(p_company_id);
npm notice run   if v_role in ('owner', 'admin') then return true; end if;
npm notice run   if public.hr_is_hr_manager(p_company_id) then return true; end if;
npm notice run   return false;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.hr_is_hr_manager(uuid) to authenticated;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.hr_can_access_sensitive_employee_fields(uuid) to authenticated;
npm notice run create or replace function public.hr_replace_employee_dependents(
npm notice run   p_company_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_employee_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_dependents jsonb
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns integer
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_count integer := 0;
npm notice run   v_item jsonb;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if not public.hr_can_access_sensitive_employee_fields(p_company_id) then
npm notice run     raise exception 'Not authorized.';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   delete from public.hr_employee_dependents
npm notice run   where company_id = p_company_id and employee_id = p_employee_id;
npm notice run   if p_dependents is null or jsonb_typeof(p_dependents) <> 'array' then
npm notice run     return 0;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   for v_item in select * from jsonb_array_elements(p_dependents)
npm notice run   loop
npm notice run     insert into public.hr_employee_dependents (
npm notice run       company_id,
npm notice run       employee_id,
npm notice run       dependent_name,
npm notice run       relationship,
npm notice run       contact_details
npm notice run     )
npm notice run     values (
npm notice run       p_company_id,
npm notice run       p_employee_id,
npm notice run       coalesce(nullif(btrim(v_item->>'dependent_name'), ''), nullif(btrim(v_item->>'name'), ''), ''),
npm notice run       coalesce(nullif(btrim(v_item->>'relationship'), ''), ''),
npm notice run       nullif(btrim(v_item->>'contact_details'), '')
npm notice run     );
npm notice run     v_count := v_count + 1;
npm notice run   end loop;
npm notice run   return v_count;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.hr_replace_employee_dependents(uuid, uuid, jsonb) to authenticated;

npm notice run safe-cloud-africa@1.0.0 npx
npm notice run 'insforge' db query create or replace function public.hr_apply_leave_approval(
npm notice run   p_leave_request_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_company_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_decision text,
npm notice run   p_actor_user_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_decline_reason text default null
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns public.hr_leave_requests
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_leave public.hr_leave_requests;
npm notice run   v_leave_type public.hr_leave_types;
npm notice run   v_year integer;
npm notice run   v_balance public.hr_leave_balances;
npm notice run   v_auto_allocated numeric(8,2);
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   select * into v_leave from public.hr_leave_requests
npm notice run     where id = p_leave_request_id and company_id = p_company_id for update;
npm notice run   if not found then raise exception 'Leave request not found.'; end if;
npm notice run   select * into v_leave_type from public.hr_leave_types where id = v_leave.leave_type_id;
npm notice run   if p_decision = 'SUPERVISOR_APPROVE' then
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests
npm notice run       set supervisor_approval_status = 'APPROVED', updated_at = now()
npm notice run       where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run   elsif p_decision = 'SUPERVISOR_DECLINE' then
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests
npm notice run       set supervisor_approval_status = 'DECLINED',
npm notice run           status = 'DECLINED',
npm notice run           decline_reason = coalesce(p_decline_reason, 'Declined by supervisor'),
npm notice run           approved_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id,
npm notice run           approved_at = now(),
npm notice run           updated_at = now()
npm notice run       where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run   elsif p_decision = 'HR_APPROVE' then
npm notice run     if v_leave_type.requires_proof and coalesce(array_length(v_leave.proof_file_ids, 1), 0) = 0 then
npm notice run       raise exception 'Proof is required.';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests
npm notice run       set hr_approval_status = 'APPROVED',
npm notice run           status = 'APPROVED',
npm notice run           approved_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id,
npm notice run           approved_at = now(),
npm notice run           updated_at = now()
npm notice run       where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run     v_year := extract(year from v_leave.start_date)::integer;
npm notice run     select * into v_balance from public.hr_leave_balances
npm notice run       where company_id = p_company_id
npm notice run         and employee_id = v_leave.employee_id
npm notice run         and leave_type_id = v_leave.leave_type_id
npm notice run         and year = v_year
npm notice run       for update;
npm notice run     if not found then
npm notice run       v_auto_allocated := greatest(coalesce(v_leave_type.default_days_per_year, 0), v_leave.total_days);
npm notice run       insert into public.hr_leave_balances
npm notice run         (company_id, employee_id, leave_type_id, year, allocated_days, used_days, updated_by_user_id)
npm notice run       values
npm notice run         (p_company_id, v_leave.employee_id, v_leave.leave_type_id, v_year, v_auto_allocated, 0, p_actor_user_id)
npm notice run       returning * into v_balance;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_balances
npm notice run       set used_days = used_days + v_leave.total_days,
npm notice run           updated_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id,
npm notice run           updated_at = now()
npm notice run       where id = v_balance.id;
npm notice run   elsif p_decision = 'HR_DECLINE' then
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests
npm notice run       set hr_approval_status = 'DECLINED',
npm notice run           status = 'DECLINED',
npm notice run           decline_reason = coalesce(p_decline_reason, 'Declined by HR'),
npm notice run           approved_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id,
npm notice run           approved_at = now(),
npm notice run           updated_at = now()
npm notice run       where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run   else
npm notice run     raise exception 'Unsupported decision';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return v_leave;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.hr_apply_leave_approval(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, text) to authenticated; --yes
Query executed successfully.
No rows returned.

npm notice run create or replace function public.hr_set_updated_at()
npm notice run returns trigger
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   new.updated_at := now();
npm notice run   return new;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run create or replace function public.hr_can_view_restricted_fields(p_company_id uuid)
npm notice run returns boolean
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run stable
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_role text;
npm notice run   v_allow_owner boolean;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if public.is_platform_admin() then return true; end if;
npm notice run   v_role := public.company_role(p_company_id);
npm notice run   if v_role = 'admin' then return true; end if;
npm notice run   if v_role = 'owner' then
npm notice run     select coalesce(owner_can_view_restricted, false) into v_allow_owner from public.hr_settings where company_id = p_company_id;
npm notice run     return coalesce(v_allow_owner, false);
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return false;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.hr_can_view_restricted_fields(uuid) to authenticated;
npm notice run create or replace function public.hr_apply_leave_approval(
npm notice run   p_leave_request_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_company_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_decision text,
npm notice run   p_actor_user_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_decline_reason text default null
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns public.hr_leave_requests
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_leave public.hr_leave_requests;
npm notice run   v_leave_type public.hr_leave_types;
npm notice run   v_year integer;
npm notice run   v_balance public.hr_leave_balances;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   select * into v_leave from public.hr_leave_requests where id = p_leave_request_id and company_id = p_company_id for update;
npm notice run   if not found then raise exception 'Leave request not found.'; end if;
npm notice run   select * into v_leave_type from public.hr_leave_types where id = v_leave.leave_type_id;
npm notice run   if p_decision = 'SUPERVISOR_APPROVE' then
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests set supervisor_approval_status = 'APPROVED', updated_at = now() where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run   elsif p_decision = 'SUPERVISOR_DECLINE' then
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests set supervisor_approval_status = 'DECLINED', status = 'DECLINED', decline_reason = coalesce(p_decline_reason, 'Declined by supervisor'), approved_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id, approved_at = now(), updated_at = now() where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run   elsif p_decision = 'HR_APPROVE' then
npm notice run     if v_leave_type.requires_proof and coalesce(array_length(v_leave.proof_file_ids, 1), 0) = 0 then raise exception 'Proof is required.'; end if;
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests set hr_approval_status = 'APPROVED', status = 'APPROVED', approved_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id, approved_at = now(), updated_at = now() where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run     v_year := extract(year from v_leave.start_date)::integer;
npm notice run     select * into v_balance from public.hr_leave_balances where company_id = p_company_id and employee_id = v_leave.employee_id and leave_type_id = v_leave.leave_type_id and year = v_year for update;
npm notice run     if not found then
npm notice run       insert into public.hr_leave_balances (company_id, employee_id, leave_type_id, year, allocated_days, used_days, updated_by_user_id)
npm notice run       values (p_company_id, v_leave.employee_id, v_leave.leave_type_id, v_year, v_leave_type.default_days_per_year, 0, p_actor_user_id)
npm notice run       returning * into v_balance;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     if v_leave.total_days > v_balance.remaining_days then raise exception 'Requested leave exceeds remaining balance.'; end if;
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_balances set used_days = used_days + v_leave.total_days, updated_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id, updated_at = now() where id = v_balance.id;
npm notice run   elsif p_decision = 'HR_DECLINE' then
npm notice run     update public.hr_leave_requests set hr_approval_status = 'DECLINED', status = 'DECLINED', decline_reason = coalesce(p_decline_reason, 'Declined by HR'), approved_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id, approved_at = now(), updated_at = now() where id = v_leave.id returning * into v_leave;
npm notice run   else
npm notice run     raise exception 'Unsupported decision';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return v_leave;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.hr_apply_leave_approval(uuid, uuid, text, uuid, text) to authenticated;

npm notice run 'insforge' db query create or replace function public.resolve_invitation_token(p_token text)

npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$

npm notice run   v_token text;
npm notice run   v_token_hash text;
npm notice run   v_row record;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   v_token := nullif(trim(p_token), '');
npm notice run   if v_token is null then
npm notice run     return query
npm notice run     select 'not_found'::text, null::uuid, null::uuid, null::text, null::text, null::text, null::text, null::timestamptz;
npm notice run     return;
npm notice run   end if;

npm notice run       token_hash = v_token_hash
npm notice run       or token = v_token
npm notice run     )
npm notice run     and status in ('PENDING', 'SENT')
npm notice run     and expires_at is not null
npm notice run     and expires_at <= now();

npm notice run     i.status,
npm notice run     i.expires_at

npm notice run   where i.token_hash = v_token_hash
npm notice run      or i.token = v_token
npm notice run   order by i.created_at desc
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run   if not found then
npm notice run     return query
npm notice run     select 'not_found'::text, null::uuid, null::uuid, null::text, null::text, null::text, null::text, null::timestamptz;
npm notice run     return;
npm notice run   end if;

npm notice run     return query

npm notice run     return;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if v_row.status = 'CANCELLED' then
npm notice run     return query
npm notice run     select 'cancelled'::text, v_row.id, v_row.company_id, v_row.company_name, v_row.email, v_row.role, v_row.status, v_row.expires_at;
npm notice run     return;
npm notice run   end if;

npm notice run     return query
npm notice run     select 'expired'::text, v_row.id, v_row.company_id, v_row.company_name, v_row.email, v_row.role, 'EXPIRED'::text, v_row.expires_at;
npm notice run     return;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if v_row.status in ('PENDING', 'SENT') then
npm notice run     return query
npm notice run     select 'ok'::text, v_row.id, v_row.company_id, v_row.company_name, v_row.email, v_row.role, v_row.status, v_row.expires_at;
npm notice run     return;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return query
npm notice run   select 'not_found'::text, v_row.id, v_row.company_id, v_row.company_name, v_row.email, v_row.role, v_row.status, v_row.expires_at;

npm notice run create or replace function public.kpi_assessment_questionnaire_rating_fn()
npm notice run returns trigger
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_weight numeric;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   new.kpi_questionnaire := coalesce(nullif(trim(new.kpi_questionnaire), ''), new.kpi_title);
npm notice run   new.kpi_title := new.kpi_questionnaire;
npm notice run   v_weight := case new.importance_rating
npm notice run     when 'high' then 2
npm notice run     when 'medium' then 1.5
npm notice run     else 1
npm notice run   end;
npm notice run   if new.manager_rating is null then
npm notice run     new.not_achieved := null;
npm notice run     new.achieved := null;
npm notice run     new.achievement_status := null;
npm notice run     new.weighted_score := null;
npm notice run   elsif new.manager_rating <= 2 then
npm notice run     new.not_achieved := true;
npm notice run     new.achieved := false;
npm notice run     new.achievement_status := 'not_achieved';
npm notice run     new.weighted_score := round((new.manager_rating::numeric * v_weight)::numeric, 2);
npm notice run   elsif new.manager_rating = 3 then
npm notice run     new.not_achieved := false;
npm notice run     new.achieved := false;
npm notice run     new.achievement_status := 'partially_achieved';
npm notice run     new.weighted_score := round((new.manager_rating::numeric * v_weight)::numeric, 2);
npm notice run   else
npm notice run     new.not_achieved := false;
npm notice run     new.achieved := true;
npm notice run     new.achievement_status := 'achieved';
npm notice run     new.weighted_score := round((new.manager_rating::numeric * v_weight)::numeric, 2);
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   new.updated_at := now();
npm notice run   return new;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run drop trigger if exists kpi_assessment_questionnaire_rating_trigger on public.kpi_assessment_lines;
npm notice run create trigger kpi_assessment_questionnaire_rating_trigger
npm notice run before insert or update of manager_rating, importance_rating, achieved, not_achieved, kpi_questionnaire, kpi_title
npm notice run on public.kpi_assessment_lines
npm notice run for each row execute function public.kpi_assessment_questionnaire_rating_fn();
npm notice run update public.kpi_assessment_lines
npm notice run set manager_rating = manager_rating;
npm notice run create or replace function public.kpi_assessment_require_ratings_before_close_fn()
npm notice run returns trigger
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_missing integer;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if new.status = 'closed' and old.status is distinct from new.status then
npm notice run     select count(*) into v_missing
npm notice run     from public.kpi_assessment_lines l
npm notice run     where l.assessment_id = new.assessment_id
npm notice run       and l.manager_rating is null;
npm notice run     if v_missing > 0 then
npm notice run       raise exception 'Manager rating is required for all KPI Questionnaires before closing assessment.';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if new.status in ('completed', 'closed') and old.status is distinct from new.status then
npm notice run     select count(*) into v_missing
npm notice run     from public.kpi_assessment_lines l
npm notice run     where l.assessment_id = new.assessment_id
npm notice run       and l.manager_rating is null;
npm notice run     if v_missing > 0 then
npm notice run       raise exception 'Manager rating is required for all KPI Questionnaires before completing assessment.';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   new.updated_at := now();
npm notice run   return new;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;

npm notice run create or replace function public.kpi_assessment_audit_assessment_fn()
npm notice run returns trigger
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
npm notice run     insert into public.kpi_assessment_audit_log(organization_id, assessment_id, event_type, old_values, new_values, actor_user_id)
npm notice run     values (new.organization_id, new.assessment_id, 'assessment_insert', null, to_jsonb(new), public.request_user_id());
npm notice run     return new;
npm notice run   elsif tg_op = 'UPDATE' then
npm notice run     insert into public.kpi_assessment_audit_log(organization_id, assessment_id, event_type, old_values, new_values, actor_user_id)
npm notice run     values (new.organization_id, new.assessment_id, 'assessment_update', to_jsonb(old), to_jsonb(new), public.request_user_id());
npm notice run     return new;
npm notice run   else
npm notice run     insert into public.kpi_assessment_audit_log(organization_id, assessment_id, event_type, old_values, new_values, actor_user_id)
npm notice run     values (old.organization_id, old.assessment_id, 'assessment_delete', to_jsonb(old), null, public.request_user_id());
npm notice run     return old;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run create or replace function public.kpi_assessment_audit_line_fn()
npm notice run returns trigger
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_org uuid;
npm notice run   v_assessment uuid;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if tg_op = 'DELETE' then
npm notice run     v_assessment := old.assessment_id;
npm notice run   else
npm notice run     v_assessment := new.assessment_id;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select a.organization_id into v_org
npm notice run   from public.kpi_assessments a
npm notice run   where a.assessment_id = v_assessment;
npm notice run   if v_org is null then
npm notice run     return coalesce(new, old);
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if tg_op = 'INSERT' then
npm notice run     insert into public.kpi_assessment_audit_log(organization_id, assessment_id, line_id, event_type, old_values, new_values, actor_user_id)
npm notice run     values (v_org, new.assessment_id, new.line_id, 'questionnaire_insert', null, to_jsonb(new), public.request_user_id());

npm notice run end;

npm notice run returns jsonb
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_uid uuid := public.request_user_id();
npm notice run   v_company_id uuid;
npm notice run   v_role text;
npm notice run   v_company_status text;
npm notice run   v_license_status text;
npm notice run   v_license_end timestamptz;
npm notice run   v_role_rank int;
npm notice run   v_best_rank int := 999;
npm notice run   v_best_company_id uuid;
npm notice run   v_best_role text;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if v_uid is null then
npm notice run     return jsonb_build_object('path', '/activate', 'reason', 'no_org');
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   for v_company_id, v_role in
npm notice run     select m.company_id, m.role
npm notice run     from public.company_memberships m
npm notice run     where m.user_id = v_uid
npm notice run       and coalesce(m.status, 'ACTIVE') = 'ACTIVE'
npm notice run   loop
npm notice run     if p_preferred_company_id is not null and v_company_id = p_preferred_company_id then
npm notice run       v_best_company_id := v_company_id;
npm notice run       v_best_role := v_role;
npm notice run       exit;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     v_role_rank := case lower(v_role)
npm notice run       when 'owner' then 1
npm notice run       when 'admin' then 2
npm notice run       when 'manager' then 3
npm notice run       when 'supervisor' then 4
npm notice run       when 'consultant' then 5
npm notice run       when 'employee' then 6
npm notice run       when 'auditor' then 7
npm notice run       else 999
npm notice run     end;
npm notice run     if v_role_rank < v_best_rank then
npm notice run       v_best_rank := v_role_rank;
npm notice run       v_best_company_id := v_company_id;
npm notice run       v_best_role := v_role;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end loop;
npm notice run   if v_best_company_id is null then
npm notice run     select c.id, 'owner'
npm notice run     into v_best_company_id, v_best_role
npm notice run     from public.companies c
npm notice run     where c.primary_admin_user_id = v_uid
npm notice run     limit 1;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if v_best_company_id is null then
npm notice run     return jsonb_build_object('path', '/activate', 'reason', 'no_org');
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select c.status
npm notice run   into v_company_status
npm notice run   from public.companies c
npm notice run   where c.id = v_best_company_id;
npm notice run   if lower(coalesce(v_company_status, 'active')) = 'suspended' then
npm notice run     return jsonb_build_object(
npm notice run       'path', '/billing/status',
npm notice run       'organizationId', v_best_company_id,
npm notice run       'reason', 'suspended'
npm notice run     );
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select l.status, l.end_date
npm notice run   into v_license_status, v_license_end
npm notice run   from public.org_licenses l
npm notice run   where l.company_id = v_best_company_id
npm notice run   order by l.end_date desc nulls last
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run   if found then
npm notice run     if lower(coalesce(v_license_status, 'active')) in ('suspended', 'expired')
npm notice run       or (v_license_end is not null and v_license_end < now()) then
npm notice run       return jsonb_build_object(
npm notice run         'path', '/billing/status',
npm notice run         'organizationId', v_best_company_id,
npm notice run         'reason', case when lower(coalesce(v_license_status, '')) = 'suspended' then 'suspended' else 'expired' end
npm notice run       );
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return jsonb_build_object(
npm notice run     'path', case lower(coalesce(v_best_role, ''))
npm notice run       when 'owner' then '/org/dashboard'
npm notice run       when 'admin' then '/admin/dashboard'
npm notice run       when 'manager' then '/manager/dashboard'
npm notice run       when 'supervisor' then '/supervisor/dashboard'
npm notice run       when 'consultant' then '/consultant/dashboard'
npm notice run       when 'employee' then '/employee/dashboard'
npm notice run       when 'auditor' then '/auditor/dashboard'
npm notice run       else '/app'
npm notice run     end,
npm notice run     'organizationId', v_best_company_id
npm notice run   );
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;

npm notice run safe-cloud-africa@1.0.0 npx
npm notice run 'insforge' db query alter table public.company_invites
npm notice run   add column if not exists department_id uuid null references public.departments(id) on delete set null;
npm notice run alter table public.company_invites
npm notice run   add column if not exists site_id uuid null references public.sites(id) on delete set null;
npm notice run create index if not exists idx_company_invites_department on public.company_invites(department_id) where department_id is not null;
npm notice run create index if not exists idx_company_invites_site on public.company_invites(site_id) where site_id is not null;
npm notice run alter table public.company_invites drop constraint if exists company_invites_status_check;
npm notice run alter table public.company_invites
npm notice run   add constraint company_invites_status_check
npm notice run   check (status in ('PENDING', 'SENT', 'FAILED', 'ACCEPTED', 'EXPIRED', 'CANCELLED'));
npm notice run comment on column public.company_invites.status is 'PENDING | SENT | FAILED | ACCEPTED | EXPIRED | CANCELLED.';
npm notice run with ranked as (
npm notice run   select
npm notice run     id,
npm notice run     row_number() over (
npm notice run       partition by company_id, lower(email)
npm notice run       order by coalesce(sent_at, created_at) desc, created_at desc, id desc
npm notice run     ) as rn
npm notice run   from public.company_invites
npm notice run   where status in ('PENDING', 'SENT')
npm notice run )
npm notice run update public.company_invites i
npm notice run set
npm notice run   status = 'CANCELLED',
npm notice run   error_message = coalesce(i.error_message, 'Auto-cancelled during invite dedupe migration.')
npm notice run from ranked r
npm notice run where i.id = r.id
npm notice run   and r.rn > 1;
npm notice run create unique index if not exists idx_company_invites_active_unique
npm notice run on public.company_invites (company_id, lower(email))
npm notice run where status in ('PENDING', 'SENT');
npm notice run create or replace function public.get_invite_id_by_token(p_token text)
npm notice run returns uuid
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_id uuid;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if nullif(trim(p_token), '') is null then
npm notice run     return null;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   update public.company_invites
npm notice run   set status = 'EXPIRED'
npm notice run   where token = trim(p_token)
npm notice run     and status in ('PENDING', 'SENT')
npm notice run     and expires_at is not null
npm notice run     and expires_at <= now();
npm notice run   select id into v_id
npm notice run   from public.company_invites
npm notice run   where token = trim(p_token)
npm notice run     and status in ('PENDING', 'SENT')
npm notice run     and (expires_at is null or expires_at > now())
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run   return v_id;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.get_invite_id_by_token(text) to anon;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.get_invite_id_by_token(text) to authenticated;
npm notice run create or replace function public.validate_invitation_token(p_token text)
npm notice run returns table(
npm notice run   invite_id uuid,
npm notice run   company_id uuid,
npm notice run   company_name text,
npm notice run   email text,
npm notice run   role text,
npm notice run   status text,
npm notice run   expires_at timestamptz
npm notice run )
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   return query
npm notice run   select
npm notice run     i.id,
npm notice run     i.company_id,
npm notice run     c.name,
npm notice run     i.email,
npm notice run     i.role,
npm notice run     i.status,
npm notice run     i.expires_at
npm notice run   from public.company_invites i
npm notice run   join public.companies c on c.id = i.company_id
npm notice run   where i.token = trim(p_token)
npm notice run     and i.status in ('PENDING', 'SENT')
npm notice run     and (i.expires_at is null or i.expires_at > now())
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.validate_invitation_token(text) to anon;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.validate_invitation_token(text) to authenticated; --yes

APPLY: ppe_issue_atomic_rpc_2026_08_21.sql
npm notice run safe-cloud-africa@1.0.0 npx
npm notice run 'insforge' db query create or replace function public.ppe_issue_and_decrement_stock(
npm notice run   p_issue jsonb,
npm notice run   p_stock_id uuid default null,
npm notice run   p_quantity integer default null,
npm notice run   p_allow_negative_stock boolean default false
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns public.ppe_issues
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_company_id uuid := (p_issue->>'company_id')::uuid;
npm notice run   v_actor_user_id uuid := (p_issue->>'issued_by_user_id')::uuid;
npm notice run   v_id uuid := coalesce(nullif(p_issue->>'id', '')::uuid, gen_random_uuid());
npm notice run   v_issue public.ppe_issues;
npm notice run   v_current_qty integer;
npm notice run   v_new_qty integer;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if v_company_id is null or v_actor_user_id is null then
npm notice run     raise exception 'company_id and issued_by_user_id are required.' using errcode = '22023';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if not (public.is_platform_admin() or public.is_company_consultant_or_admin(v_company_id)) then
npm notice run     raise exception 'Not authorized to issue PPE for this company.' using errcode = '42501';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   insert into public.ppe_issues
npm notice run   select * from jsonb_populate_record(null::public.ppe_issues, p_issue || jsonb_build_object('id', v_id))
npm notice run   returning * into v_issue;
npm notice run   if p_stock_id is not null then
npm notice run     if p_quantity is null or p_quantity <= 0 then
npm notice run       raise exception 'Quantity must be a positive number.' using errcode = '22023';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     select on_hand_qty into v_current_qty
npm notice run     from public.ppe_stock
npm notice run     where id = p_stock_id and company_id = v_company_id
npm notice run     for update;
npm notice run     if not found then
npm notice run       raise exception 'PPE stock record not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     v_new_qty := v_current_qty - p_quantity;
npm notice run     if v_new_qty < 0 and not p_allow_negative_stock then
npm notice run       raise exception 'Insufficient stock for this issue.' using errcode = 'P0001';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run     update public.ppe_stock
npm notice run     set on_hand_qty = v_new_qty,
npm notice run         updated_at = now(),
npm notice run         updated_by_user_id = v_actor_user_id
npm notice run     where id = p_stock_id and company_id = v_company_id;
npm notice run     insert into public.ppe_stock_movements (
npm notice run       company_id, stock_id, movement_type, quantity, reference_type, reference_id,
npm notice run       ppe_issue_id, old_on_hand_qty, new_on_hand_qty, created_by_user_id, created_at
npm notice run     ) values (
npm notice run       v_company_id, p_stock_id, 'out', p_quantity, 'ppe_issue', v_issue.id,
npm notice run       v_issue.id, v_current_qty, v_new_qty, v_actor_user_id, now()
npm notice run     );
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return v_issue;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run comment on function public.ppe_issue_and_decrement_stock is
npm notice run   'Atomically inserts a ppe_issues row and (when a stock record is given) decrements ppe_stock + inserts the matching ppe_stock_movements row, all in one transaction. Replaces the previous insert-then-compensating-delete approach in createPpeIssue().';
npm notice run grant execute on function public.ppe_issue_and_decrement_stock(jsonb, uuid, integer, boolean) to authenticated; --yes
Query executed successfully.
No rows returned.
  OK: ppe_issue_atomic_rpc_2026_08_21.sql

APPLY: ppe_stock_movement_atomic_rpc_2026_08_20.sql
npm notice run safe-cloud-africa@1.0.0 npx
npm notice run 'insforge' db query create or replace function public.ppe_apply_stock_movement(
npm notice run   p_company_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_stock_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_movement_type text,
npm notice run   p_quantity integer,
npm notice run   p_actor_user_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_reason text default null,
npm notice run   p_reference_type text default null,
npm notice run   p_reference_id uuid default null,
npm notice run   p_ppe_issue_id uuid default null,
npm notice run   p_transaction_date date default null,
npm notice run   p_allow_negative_stock boolean default false
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns table (
npm notice run   stock_id uuid,
npm notice run   new_on_hand_qty integer,
npm notice run   movement_id uuid
npm notice run )
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_current_qty integer;
npm notice run   v_new_qty integer;
npm notice run   v_movement_id uuid;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if p_quantity is null or p_quantity <= 0 then
npm notice run     raise exception 'Quantity must be a positive number.' using errcode = '22023';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if not (public.is_platform_admin() or public.is_company_consultant_or_admin(p_company_id)) then
npm notice run     raise exception 'Not authorized to modify PPE stock for this company.' using errcode = '42501';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select on_hand_qty into v_current_qty
npm notice run   from public.ppe_stock
npm notice run   where id = p_stock_id and company_id = p_company_id
npm notice run   for update;
npm notice run   if not found then
npm notice run     raise exception 'PPE stock record not found.' using errcode = 'P0002';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if p_movement_type in ('in', 'return') then
npm notice run     v_new_qty := v_current_qty + p_quantity;
npm notice run   elsif p_movement_type = 'out' then
npm notice run     v_new_qty := v_current_qty - p_quantity;
npm notice run     if v_new_qty < 0 and not p_allow_negative_stock then
npm notice run       raise exception 'Insufficient stock for this movement.' using errcode = 'P0001';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   elsif p_movement_type = 'adjust' then
npm notice run     v_new_qty := p_quantity;
npm notice run   elsif p_movement_type = 'ordered' then
npm notice run     v_new_qty := v_current_qty;
npm notice run   elsif p_movement_type in ('damage', 'expired') then
npm notice run     v_new_qty := v_current_qty - p_quantity;
npm notice run     if v_new_qty < 0 and not p_allow_negative_stock then
npm notice run       raise exception 'Insufficient stock for this write-off.' using errcode = 'P0001';
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   else
npm notice run     raise exception 'Unknown movement type: %', p_movement_type using errcode = '22023';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   update public.ppe_stock
npm notice run   set on_hand_qty = v_new_qty,
npm notice run       updated_at = now(),
npm notice run       updated_by_user_id = p_actor_user_id
npm notice run   where id = p_stock_id and company_id = p_company_id;
npm notice run   insert into public.ppe_stock_movements (
npm notice run     company_id, stock_id, movement_type, quantity, reason, reference_type, reference_id,
npm notice run     ppe_issue_id, old_on_hand_qty, new_on_hand_qty, created_by_user_id, created_at, transaction_date
npm notice run   ) values (
npm notice run     p_company_id, p_stock_id, p_movement_type, p_quantity, p_reason, p_reference_type, p_reference_id,
npm notice run     p_ppe_issue_id, v_current_qty, v_new_qty, p_actor_user_id, now(), p_transaction_date
npm notice run   )
npm notice run   returning id into v_movement_id;
npm notice run   return query select p_stock_id, v_new_qty, v_movement_id;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run comment on function public.ppe_apply_stock_movement is
npm notice run   'Atomically updates ppe_stock.on_hand_qty and inserts the matching ppe_stock_movements row in one transaction -- replaces the previous two-separate-REST-write approach in createPpeStockMovement().';
npm notice run grant execute on function public.ppe_apply_stock_movement(
npm notice run   uuid, uuid, text, integer, uuid, text, text, uuid, uuid, date, boolean

APPLY: rbac_consultant_scope_and_trial_export.sql
npm notice run safe-cloud-africa@1.0.0 npx
npm notice run 'insforge' db query create or replace function public.consultant_scope_permits(
npm notice run   p_company_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_module text default null,
npm notice run   p_department_id uuid default null,
npm notice run   p_site_id uuid default null
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns boolean
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run stable
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   m record;
npm notice run   scope jsonb;
npm notice run   expires_at timestamptz;
npm notice run   allowed_modules text[];
npm notice run   allowed_departments uuid[];
npm notice run   allowed_sites uuid[];
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   select role, consultant_scope into m
npm notice run   from public.company_memberships
npm notice run   where company_id = p_company_id
npm notice run     and user_id = public.request_user_id()
npm notice run     and status = 'ACTIVE'
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run   if not found then
npm notice run     return false;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if m.role not in ('consultant','auditor') then
npm notice run     return true;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   scope := m.consultant_scope;
npm notice run   if scope is null then
npm notice run     return false;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   expires_at := (scope->>'expiresAt')::timestamptz;
npm notice run   if expires_at is not null and now() > expires_at then
npm notice run     return false;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if p_module is not null then
npm notice run     allowed_modules := array(select jsonb_array_elements_text(scope->'allowedModules'));
npm notice run     if allowed_modules is not null and array_length(allowed_modules, 1) > 0
npm notice run        and not (p_module = any(allowed_modules)) then
npm notice run       return false;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if p_department_id is not null then
npm notice run     allowed_departments := array(select (x->>0)::uuid from jsonb_array_elements(scope->'allowedDepartments') x);
npm notice run     if allowed_departments is not null and array_length(allowed_departments, 1) > 0
npm notice run        and not (p_department_id = any(allowed_departments)) then
npm notice run       return false;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if p_site_id is not null then
npm notice run     allowed_sites := array(select (x->>0)::uuid from jsonb_array_elements(scope->'allowedSites') x);
npm notice run     if allowed_sites is not null and array_length(allowed_sites, 1) > 0
npm notice run        and not (p_site_id = any(allowed_sites)) then
npm notice run       return false;
npm notice run     end if;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   return true;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run comment on function public.consultant_scope_permits(uuid, text, uuid, uuid) is 'True if current user is active in company and (not consultant/auditor or scope allows module/dept/site and not expired).';
npm notice run grant execute on function public.consultant_scope_permits(uuid, text, uuid, uuid) to authenticated;
npm notice run create or replace function public.can_company_export(p_company_id uuid)
npm notice run returns boolean
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run stable
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   in_trial boolean;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if public.is_platform_admin() then
npm notice run     return true;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   if not exists (
npm notice run     select 1 from public.company_memberships m
npm notice run     where m.company_id = p_company_id and m.user_id = public.request_user_id() and m.status = 'ACTIVE'
npm notice run   ) then
npm notice run     return false;
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select (ol.is_trial = true and (ol.trial_ends_at is null or ol.trial_ends_at >= current_date))
npm notice run   into in_trial
npm notice run   from public.org_licenses ol
npm notice run   where ol.company_id = p_company_id and ol.status = 'active'
npm notice run   order by ol.end_date desc
npm notice run   limit 1;
npm notice run   return coalesce(in_trial, false) = false;
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run comment on function public.can_company_export(uuid) is 'True if current user can export (download) for this company; false when subscription is in trial.';
npm notice run grant execute on function public.can_company_export(uuid) to authenticated; --yes

npm notice run safe-cloud-africa@1.0.0 npx
npm notice run 'insforge' db query create or replace function public.set_platform_sellable_feature_lock(
npm notice run   p_company_id uuid,
npm notice run   p_feature_key text,
npm notice run   p_locked boolean
npm notice run )
npm notice run returns jsonb
npm notice run language plpgsql
npm notice run security definer
npm notice run set search_path = public
npm notice run as $$
npm notice run declare
npm notice run   v_metadata jsonb;
npm notice run   v_features jsonb;
npm notice run   v_feature jsonb;
npm notice run   v_actor uuid;
npm notice run begin
npm notice run   if not public.is_platform_admin() then
npm notice run     raise exception 'forbidden' using errcode = '42501';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   v_actor := auth.uid();
npm notice run   if v_actor is null then
npm notice run     raise exception 'unauthorized' using errcode = '42501';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   select metadata into v_metadata
npm notice run   from public.companies
npm notice run   where id = p_company_id
npm notice run   for update;
npm notice run   if not found then
npm notice run     raise exception 'company not found' using errcode = 'P0002';
npm notice run   end if;
npm notice run   v_metadata := coalesce(v_metadata, '{}'::jsonb);
npm notice run   v_features := coalesce(v_metadata -> 'sellable_features', '{}'::jsonb);
npm notice run   v_feature := coalesce(v_features -> p_feature_key, '{"enabled": true, "locked": true}'::jsonb);
npm notice run   v_feature := jsonb_set(
npm notice run     jsonb_set(
npm notice run       v_feature,
npm notice run       '{enabled}',
npm notice run       to_jsonb(coalesce((v_feature ->> 'enabled')::boolean, true))
npm notice run     ),
npm notice run     '{locked}',
npm notice run     to_jsonb(p_locked)
npm notice run   );
npm notice run   v_features := jsonb_set(v_features, array[p_feature_key], v_feature, true);
npm notice run   v_metadata := jsonb_set(v_metadata, '{sellable_features}', v_features, true);
npm notice run   update public.companies
npm notice run   set metadata = v_metadata
npm notice run   where id = p_company_id;
npm notice run   insert into public.platform_admin_audit_logs (
npm notice run     actor_user_id,
npm notice run     action,
npm notice run     target_company_id,
npm notice run     details
npm notice run   ) values (
npm notice run     v_actor,
npm notice run     case when p_locked then 'sellable_feature_locked' else 'sellable_feature_unlocked' end,
npm notice run     p_company_id,
npm notice run     jsonb_build_object('feature_key', p_feature_key, 'locked', p_locked)
npm notice run   );
npm notice run   return jsonb_build_object(
npm notice run     'company_id', p_company_id,
npm notice run     'feature_key', p_feature_key,
npm notice run     'locked', p_locked,
npm notice run     'metadata', v_metadata
npm notice run   );
npm notice run end;
npm notice run $$;
npm notice run grant execute on function public.set_platform_sellable_feature_lock(uuid, text, boolean) to authenticated;
npm notice run NOTIFY pgrst, 'reload schema'; --yes
